# FOUNDRY belt bridge sweep 2026-10-08
Bridge held by FOUNDRY since 02:16Z. Game was DOWN at start.

## Milestones

### STOP-WORK REPORT (what FOUNDRY touched live before the owner's stop order, ~19:24-19:27 PDT)
- Bridge was held by FOUNDRY at start; game read DOWN, then `./game` measured RimWorldWin64.exe RUNNING/LOADING (someone else's launch; Player.log mid-load of workshop patches).
- I RAN `taskkill /F /IM RimWorldWin64.exe` (PID 1448). That was very likely BENCH's Giant Trees review load. It is dead; BENCH must relaunch.
- Deploy pass 1 (game running): plain `--apply` wrote 76 files; 2 DLLs failed (ShipVermin, StructureInjections, Errno 22). `--compose biomes --apply` wrote 27 DLLs. Logs: Transient/belt_deploy_plain_20261008.txt, belt_deploy_biomes_20261008.txt.
- Deploy pass 2 (after kill): plain `--apply` partial (4 "!" lines), biomes REFUSED, nothing written (28 "!" lines). Logs: *_plain2_*, *_biomes2_*.
- NOT touched: ModsConfig.xml, Steam, modset_builder, bridge tools. No ledger writes beyond the pre-existing dirty FOUNDRY.jsonl.
- Net: Mods folder now holds today's repo state for most mods (incl. the 27 biome DLLs); BENCH's list may load newer content than it expected.

## Offline prep for the next run
Order: (1) `rimflow bridge take`; ./game down; kill game FIRST. (2) `deploy_custom_mods.py --apply`, then `--compose biomes --apply` (check the biomes refusal text in belt_deploy_biomes2 first). (3) `modset_builder.py --tier acc_harness --apply` (BRIDGE, gimmesomeslack, luminouspigment, wreckedmachines, droidworks, falllinearrivals, unfinishedline, rut.patches, titaniccreatures; dlc on), launch via Steam. (4) `rimflow next --acceptance --seat FOUNDRY`: L1 reads first (20 items), then L2 on one quicktest map, `rimflow verify --criterion`. (5) `modcheck run` x5 mods; (6) flowworks `validation_v2.py --live --fresh-map` on the flowworks tier (expect 59/0/0). (7) belt_watchdog.py every 5 min.

Decision strings (Player.log): "Bridge token:" = up; first exception = first `Exception`/`NullReferenceException` line, not the loudest; `0 MakeThing stuff=null` after WhisperSarlaccSign (LIVE_ROUND2 A3); `[RM CreatureBehaviors] instant job loop` (A2); no `ConfigError` for RUT_EmpireRungDef (EMPIRE_ESCALATION A4).

Criteria-to-check (from rimflow next --acceptance, first items):
- MOD_OPTIONS_RETROFIT_1 A1 L1 full-list Mod Settings opens no red errors (needs full list, not acc_harness); A2/A3 L2.
- JAWA_SWIM_HOOD_KEEP_1 A1 L1 state-read Apparel_Head.CanDrawNow on swimming hooded Jawa; A2 L2 gauntlet; A3 L4 owner.

# PART 2 (2026-10-08, offline prep, nothing run live): the remaining 12 L1 items + all 108 L2 items

Coverage: L1 items 10-21 (12) + L2 (108) = 120 items, 1 to 4 owed criteria each; the first 9 L1 items are above. Sections below are per chunk, written by read-only helpers from `rimflow show`, mod XML/C# and `Transient/modcheck/*.json`. Treat any "UNVERIFIED"/"guess" a helper flagged as unmeasured. Rules for every check: `jawa/get_defs` takes `defs` as a STRING "DefType/DefName" and the result's `success`/`foundCount`/`notFound` are what to read; wrap any python.exe output in `tr -d '\r\n'`; bridge calls run only under python.exe (not rimbridge_client.py from WSL).

## Proposed sitting order (one cold load per tier; tier chosen by dominant tier in each item's section, heuristic)
1. `acc_biomes` (+gimmesomeslack): ~52 items, mostly composed `mandrake.rm.biomes` (Cathedral, Forge, Webwork, Warscar, LongShade, Sump, Miasma, Wasteland, Stillsand-lite, FIRST_SCRIPT items). L1 reads first, then L2 on one quicktest map.
2. `acc_green_min` then `acc_green_min2` (same base, adds swbestiary/alphaanimals/mlie; min2 adds LuminousPigment+Ninefold+PyrelandsMechanics): Stillsand, Greentide, Deeps, Abyss, Scarlands, ShipVermin, Titanic, Warcasket, Gizka, OasisMaker, RiverColors, Shokkweave (about 40 items). Could be a single merged tier.
3. `acc_l1x` + `acc_harness` (WreckedMachines, Droidworks, FallLine, UnfinishedLine, AcousticScanner, ShipShields, Rakatan, Mindstone): ~12 items.
4. `flowworks` (+gimmesomeslack; bridge/Bazaar/LiquidBottle/River weirs/pits/ladder/container/NorthStar baseline, explosiveknockback and kineticarms variants): ~20 items.
5. Small per-mod tiers, one load each: `bacta`, `warlab`, `diving`, `weepingstones`, `graffiti`, `pyrelands`, `bench` (Armoury).
6. NEW TIER NEEDED (36 criterion mentions; HuttSlavePit, CathedralRegardBlackboard, Kyber, PropaneLake, ScavengerEvents, Bazaar, AssailantSalvage, BrainWorms, MechanoidPass, Aerosol, etc.): build tiers in `modset_builder.py` offline first; names are in the sections.
7. Full 630-mod list (only MOD_OPTIONS_RETROFIT_1 A1, LASSO A3/A4, full-list log items): last, owner-authorized load.
8. L4/L3 owner-judgement criteria need no load; list them to the owner.

## Section 0
# Section 0 (offline research; nothing run)

Common: bridge calls only via python.exe (WSL cannot reach loopback); strip CRLF with `tr -d '\r\n'`. modcheck run REWRITES ModsConfig (swaps to MINIMAL) - Charter expensive-list action. Chain driver for all scene criteria: `python3 -m modcheck.cli run <Mod>` from src/RimMandrake/Utils (Desktop only). Scenes: `jawa/static_call type=<PROOF> method=Stage args="<scene>,x,z"` then `rimworld/step_game_ticks ticks=300` then `method=Verdict args="<scene>"`; PASS prefix = pass, INVALID = UNMEASURED (never PASS).

## BACTA_REVIVAL_MECHANIC_1
Mod: Bacta (`mandrake.rsw.bacta`); item is BLOCKED on 3 live findings (2026-09-25) and `git log -- src/RimStarWars/Bacta/Source` shows NO commit after `dff008054` (the original revival build) except unrelated settings/csproj edits, so none of findings 1-3 has a code fix yet: all three criteria below need code or a design ruling BEFORE a live run can pass. Tier: existing `bacta` (setting `revivalEnabled` must be set true through `jawa/mod_settings_field`, default false). Harness: `Bacta/validation.py` chain `revival` (components `revival_off_by_default_refuses_corpse`, `revival_window_refuses_old_corpse`, `revival_on_revives_into_the_tank`), which drives revival with `jawa/ordered_job` (forced) not the WorkGiver.
- **A1 L2** (revival WorkGiver fires automatically on a suitable corpse).
  1. Deciding: kill a Colonist (`jawa/damage`), set `revivalEnabled=true`, keep an idle colonist with Hauling enabled, step ~2000-3000 ticks WITHOUT `jawa/prioritized_work` or `ordered_job`; then `jawa/list_things group=Corpse` is empty and `jawa/list_pawns` shows the victim alive/in tank, and the worker's `curJob` during the window == `RSW_CarryCorpseToBactaTank`. State read: WorkGiverDef `RSW_CarryCorpseToBactaTank` (workType Hauling, priorityInType 92, requiredCapacities Manipulation) - `jawa/get_defs defs="WorkGiverDef/RSW_CarryCorpseToBactaTank"`.
  2. Expected: job taken by itself. 3. Tier `bacta`. 4. NOT decided; 2026-09-25 about 2000 ticks produced no auto pickup, cause "not isolated" (candidates to read first: Hauling work-type priority on the quicktest colonist, WorkGiver ordering vs vanilla `HaulCorpses`/burial taking the corpse first, `corpse.IsForbidden`, HasJobOnThing's `CanReserveAndReach(... Danger.Deadly)`, `ShouldSkip` runs while revivalEnabled). Add a diagnostic static_call (HasJobOnThing result) before spending a load.
  5. Traps: forced `prioritized_work` bypasses the scanner and hides this; the validation's carry() is forced too.
- **A2 L2** (vital-organ death revives then dies again; outcome matches owner design).
  1. Deciding (after the design call): kill by heart destruction (`jawa/damage` on Heart), revive via the tank, read `jawa/list_pawns` dead + `jawa/letter_list` "Death:" letter; hediffs `MissingBodyPart Heart` and `ClinicalDeathNoHeartbeat` rising (0.13 -> 0.83 observed).
  2. Expected: per the owner's design (not yet recorded: either CanAcceptCorpse refuses a corpse missing a vital part, or never-regrow gets a vital-organ exception). 3. `bacta`. 4. BLOCKED on a design call - `rimflow` ask the owner by question card (2 options with trade-offs). Currently CanAcceptCorpse (Building_BactaTank.cs ~273-320) has no vital-part test.
  5. Do not pick a side autonomously (design call).
- **A3 L2** (after TryResurrect clears wounds, the tank does not eject with 'nothing to heal').
  1. Deciding: revive a corpse (cuts only), then read `jawa/inspect_string` on the tank after >=1 healing pass (250 ticks) and `jawa/list_pawns`: occupant still contained (`_contained`) rather than ejected, bacta consumed ~0 is OK, and no "nothing to heal" message in `rimworld/list_messages`.
  2. Expected: occupant retained (or the new designed behaviour stated). 3. `bacta`. 4. NOT decided: needs code (autoEject skips a freshly revived occupant for a grace period) or a ruling that eject is fine; the validation's own comment accepts "released at the next 250-tick pass".
  5. autoEjectEnabled=false control isolates the cause.
- Tier: `bacta`; all three L2 quicktest; none decidable until code/design change (A1 cause unknown, A2 design, A3 code).

## CATHEDRAL_STAGE_HUM_BRIDGE_1
Mod: RustCathedral Hum kit (`mandrake.rm.rustcathedral`, composed in `mandrake.rm.biomes`; classes `RimMandrake.RustCathedral.Hum.RM_MapComponent_BiomeAttitude` with `SetStage/GetStage/GetBand(Map)` statics and Scribed `stage`; def `RM_RustCathedralAttitude` stageParams: stage 0 WARY bandCeiling 0, decay x1; stage 1/2 no ceiling, decay x1.5). Bridge tool exists: `jawa/cathedral_attitude_set_stage stage=0|1|2` (JawaBenchCathedralAttitudeTools.cs; returns success, present, stage read back, displayBand, rawBand, ticksGame; `present=false` is not an error - no map / mod not loaded / biome has no attitude def). Harness: RustCathedral/validation.py (hum chain uses `jawa/static_call type=RimMandrake.RustCathedral.Hum.RM_RustCathedralHumProof method=ProofLadder|ProofDrain|ProofWatched`); `Transient/modcheck/RustCathedral_20261007T100939Z.json` exists; no acceptance_map row for this item (the RustCathedral.json table covers RUSTCATHEDRAL_BASE_FINISH_BUILD_1 only, all UNMAPPED). Tier: **acc_biomes**; L2 quicktest map retiled to RM_RustCathedral.
Key trap (from the 10-07 sweep): the attitude comp BINDS its def at mapgen (or inside a *Proof* method) - on a retiled quicktest map `GetBand` reads -1 until bound, so a stage call alone shows `present=true` but rawBand/displayBand -1. Bind first with a Proof call (`ProofLadder` binds and drives `ProofBandAt`, then resets), or generate a real Cathedral map.
- **A1 L2** (WARY/TOLERATED hold the ceiling and show recovery afterwards).
  1. Deciding: (a) `jawa/static_call ... RM_RustCathedralHumProof method=ProofLadder` to bind and raise the raw band (composite 80 -> high band); (b) `jawa/cathedral_attitude_set_stage stage=0` -> expect `stage:0`, `displayBand:0` while `rawBand >= 1`; (c) `stage=1` -> expect `displayBand == rawBand` (ceiling lifted); (d) recovery: drop irritation and step ticks (`rimworld/step_game_ticks` / `jawa/time_set_ticks`), read `rawBand`/`displayBand` repeatedly: decay at stage 1 about 1.5x the stage-0 rate (decayRateMultiplier). "Dark flip back to WARY regardless of history" = set 0 again after 2 and see ceiling 0. Optionally cross-check `jawa/get_defs defs="RM_BiomeAttitudeDef/RM_RustCathedralAttitude"` (class RM_BiomeAttitudeDef is namespaced; the validation notes `get_defs` may not take the type name -> UNMEASURED, use the def XML).
  2. Expected: ceiling 0 at WARY, none at TOLERATED, faster recovery 1.5x; raw irritation/goodwill/hysteresis unchanged (selftest `AttitudeFuzz.cs`/`RM_AttitudeKernel` covers the pure part offline: `kit selftests green` is A3, already L0).
  3. Tier acc_biomes. 4. Not offline-decided for the live tool path; kernel clamp logic is fuzzed offline. Needs binding step + decay sampling (new component). 5. Traps: GetBand -1 when unbound is NOT a failure; hysteresis bounds untouched; use python.exe; restore stage to 0.
- **A2 L2** (baseline hum state persists unchanged across save/load).
  1. Deciding: set stage 1, `rimworld/save_game` to a NEW scratch slot name, confirm a NEW file appeared and no existing .rws changed size (save_game has silently written the CURRENT slot instead of `saveName`), `rimworld/load_game_ready` (or load the scratch save), then `jawa/static_call type=RimMandrake.RustCathedral.Hum.RM_MapComponent_BiomeAttitude method=GetStage` (needs a Map arg - or call `cathedral_attitude_set_stage` with the same stage and compare, which writes) and `GetBand`; expect stage 1 and displayBand unchanged. `Scribe_Values.Look(ref stage,"stage",0)` makes the field persist, so the only risk is the component rebinding after load.
  2. Expected: stage and displayBand equal pre-save. 3. acc_biomes. 4. Not decided; no existing component. The cold-ish load cycle is the only expensive part (quicktest world load of a small mod list ~ seconds to minutes). 5. Never overwrite the canonical start save; back up Saves keepers first and stat after; a save is the one genuinely risky act here.
- Tier: acc_biomes; A1/A2 L2 quicktest map (A2 needs a save+load cycle, so a bit above plain L2).

Proposed safe instrument: add a tendable immunity disease (e.g. `Flu`/`Malaria` HediffDef, low severity) via `jawa/pawn_health`, immerse, and read ImmunityRecord via `jawa/pawn_get` / `jawa/static_call` into `BactaHealingUtility` (immunityGain = immunityGainPerDay * days * droidMult, applied only "where medicine could have helped"); compare against `infectionAssistEnabled=false` control. Expected delta ~ immunityGainPerDay * ticks/60000.
  2. Expected: immunity rises faster than the no-tank baseline by the tuned rate; no extra with the toggle off. 3. Tier `bacta`. 4. Needs a new component (and a decision that Flu/Malaria is an acceptable stand-in for "infected wound"; otherwise a natural-infection route with a long wait).
  5. Never add WoundInfection.
- **A3 L2** (old scar erases after tuned immersion).
  1. Components `tank_scar/scar_erasure_off_keeps_the_scar` and `tank_scar/scar_erasure_on_removes_the_scar` (`scarHealPerDay`); scar = `Scar` hediff via `jawa/pawn_health`; read hediff list after N ticks. 2. Expected: scar present with scarErasureEnabled=false, gone after the tuned time with it on. 3. `bacta`. 4. Component exists; one live `modcheck run Bacta`.
- **A4 L2** (fluid drains with use and blocks use at zero).
  1. Components `tank_heals/drains_bacta_while_healing` (used 0.05..1.0 over 2500 ticks; expected ~0.21 at fluidCostPerDay=5), `tank_needs_and_dry/dry_tank_holds_occupant_when_autoeject_off`, `.dry_tank_ejects_when_autoeject_on`; fuel read via `jawa/inspect_string` "Bacta: n / 30". "Blocks further use at zero": `Building_BactaTank.CanAcceptPawn` refuses (`RSW_BactaTankReportNoFluid`) - read via a new enter attempt on the empty tank (`jawa/ordered_job EnterBuilding`, job ends). 2. As numbers above. 3. `bacta`. 4. Drain + dry-eject components exist; the explicit "refuses new entry at zero" is not a component.
- **A5 L2** (each of the 6 toggles off disables exactly its effect).
  1. Chains: `tank_heals/enters_and_reports_off` (healingEnabled), `tank_scar/scar_erasure_off_keeps_the_scar` (scarErasureEnabled), `tank_needs_and_dry/needs_held_while_immersed` (suspendNeedsEnabled), `.dry_tank_holds_occupant_when_autoeject_off` (autoEjectEnabled), `tank_droid/droid_toggle_off_stops_the_assist` (medicalDroidEnabled), `field_patch/patch_unusable_when_field_items_off` (fieldItemsEnabled), `revival/revival_off_by_default_refuses_corpse` (revivalEnabled), plus `settings_roundtrip/*_round_trips`. infectionAssistEnabled has NO component (see A2). Which "6" the criterion means is not defined: item says "all 6 settings toggles"; the file has 8 bools, so ask FOUNDRY to pin the six.
  2. Expected: each off => only its effect disappears. 3. `bacta`. 4. Mostly coverable by one `modcheck run Bacta`; infection toggle is the gap.

Expected per spec (needs Joy removed after the downgrade because SetTier calls AddOrRemoveNeedsAsAppropriate; break fires/blocked per tier: blank/mindless `blocksMentalBreaks`). 3. acc_harness. 4. Not decided; component needed. 5. Mental breaks are stochastic; use force_mental_break to test the gate not the roll.
- **A3 L2** (blank-tier droid is inert; box 4).

Candidate: one component to record; then `rimflow verify`.

## LIQUID_BOTTLE_LOOP_1
Owning mod `mandrake.rm.flowworks` (NOT in composed biomes). Tier `flowworks` (rbs + flowworks + gimmesomeslack) already loads it; FlowWorks "extensions" suite = `python.exe src/RimMandrake/FlowWorks/northstar/extension_proof.py --live --chains <c,...>` (bridge held, `flowworks` tier, Playing quicktest map; `--list` offline). Results beside it: `extension_result_<stamp>.json`. Defs: `RM_Bottle_FreshWater`, `RM_Bottle_BoilingWater`, `RM_BottleEmpty`, `RM_BottleDirty`, `RM_LiquidTank`; settings `bottleLoopEnabled`, `bottleDirtyStageEnabled`, `bottleRevertEnabled`, `tankLoopEnabled`.
- **A1 (L2)** fill from shore -> drink -> dirty -> wash -> empty. Existing chain `toggle_bottle_loop/bottle_loop_on` only spawns tank+filled bottle+colonist and checks a `RM_Bottle_FreshWater` still lists after 20 pulses (state only, does NOT see a shore fill, drink, dirty or wash). `toggle_bottle_dirty/bottle_dirty_on` has NO assertion (just `_wait`). Deciding check = NEW chain: place `RM_BottleEmpty` 5 cells from `WaterShallow` (jawa/set_terrain), fed colonist, `jawa/ordered_job`/`prioritized_work` fill (root cause of the 2026-09-25 failure fixed at cf1e75d26: JobDrivers failed on 'despawned' carried container), `jawa/list_things defName=RM_Bottle_FreshWater` expect >=1 (before fix: 0 over 4 tries); then drink job -> expect `RM_BottleDirty` count 1 (needs bottleDirtyStageEnabled true) -> wash job -> expect `RM_BottleEmpty` count 1 and `RM_BottleDirty` 0. Log string to rule out: first exception in Player.log naming `JobDriver_FillBottle`. Pass = all three transitions observed in order.
- **A2 (L2)** boiling bottle becomes fresh + blood bottle rots, hemopack beats clock. HALF OFFLINE-EVIDENCED: chain `bottle_revert/boiling_bottle_reverts_to_fresh` + `revert_off_holds` (3 boiling -> after 2750 ticks 0 boiling / 3 fresh; expect "want 0 / 3") PASSED in `src/RimMandrake/FlowWorks/northstar/extension_result_20261006T040417.json`; re-run on the current build before `rimflow verify`. Blood half is IMPOSSIBLE as written: item note 2026-10-06 says blood row NOT built (no fill source for bottled blood; BLOCKED on an owner call) - `RM_Bottle_Blood*` does not exist. => split the criterion or get the owner ruling first.
- **A3 (L2)** settings off -> no dirty bottles, loop whole. Existing `toggle_bottle_dirty/bottle_dirty_off` and `toggle_bottle_loop/bottle_loop_off` assert nothing (`_wait` only). Deciding check (NEW): set `bottleDirtyStageEnabled=false` via `jawa/mod_settings_field` (python.exe; restore in finally), repeat the A1 loop; expect `jawa/list_things defName=RM_BottleDirty` == 0 map-wide at the end AND `RM_BottleEmpty` count 1 (drink returns clean empty). Control: same loop with it true yields 1 dirty (A1).
Offline-decided? NO (A2 revert half has a recorded PASS in a Transient result only; not a modcheck/acceptance_map row). Traps: undrafted colonists haul test bottles out of the area (chain drafts them); leftover fresh bottle gets emptied into the tank; python.exe CRLF; cold full list is ~15 min, `flowworks` tier ~ seconds.
Summary: tier flowworks; all three L2 quicktest map; A1/A3 need a new chain, A2 blood half blocked.

## FEVERWOOD_BOUGH_SOIL_TERRAIN_1
Owning mod `mandrake.rm.feverwood` (wave 2), folded into composed `mandrake.rm.biomes`; terrain `RUT_BoughSoil` (fertility 1.4, `src/RimMandrake/FeverWood/Defs/TerrainDefs/RUT_BoughSoil.xml`). Today's `bough_soil/bough_soil_fertile_for_every_crown_plant` (validation.py L1025) only does `jawa/get_defs defs="TerrainDef/RUT_BoughSoil" fields="fertility,affordances"` and compares fertility >= max `fertilityMin` of CROWN_PLANTS (parsed from roster) and GrowSoil present, Heavy absent. That is an L1 def read, NOT rooting. Tier: NEW TIER NEEDED: brrainz.rimbridgeserver, lee.theforce.lightsaber (donor of `Force_KyberCrystal`, plus its dependency closure as in the `bench` tier), mandrake.rut.patches (`RUT_Mindstone`), mandrake.rm.biomes (RustCathedral biome/faction), mandrake.rm.gimmesomeslack. `bench` has lightsaber but is MISSING mandrake.jawa.patches; `acc_green_min` has biomes+rut.patches but not the lightsaber donor.
- **A1 (L2)** live kyber sale raises Heat. Check: start the script with `--polls >=6`; between poll 1 and 2 a driver `rimworld/spawn_thing` N x `Force_KyberCrystal` then (poll 3) removes them (destroy / real sale to a trader); script counts `jawa/list_things defName=Force_KyberCrystal` -> `countMatched` DECREASE. Expected in the JSONL: a record whose heat value goes from 0 to > 0 (sublinear `_sale_heat`), and `hutt_interest` > 0; no `would_fire` of a mutation. Honest gap in the item: the kyber/Heat path has NEVER been hit live (0 kyber on the campaign). Trap: the count only RISES on spawn, so heat must come from the later decrease; a spawn alone proves nothing. Read the JSONL record fields, not the console summary.
- **A2 (L2)** Cathedral-ground signal in log. Check: a map whose `jawa/map_info` `mapBiome` (or `mapParent.label`) contains "cathedral" (RM_RustCathedral tile / settlement); expected JSONL `cathedral_ground_confidence == "heuristic-label-match"` (else `"no-marker-found"` = FAIL). A quicktest map takes its biome from the start tile, so it needs either `rimworld-world-editing` (`jawa/world_*` set tile biome, then a start there) or the owner's live campaign on that tile; a plain quicktest map cannot produce it. Heuristic is label-substring only (documented gap), so PASS proves the log works, not geolocation.
Summary: NEW TIER (rbs+lightsaber donor+rut.patches+biomes+gss); A1 L2 quicktest map + driver; A2 L2 needs a Cathedral-biome map.

Generic bridge reads: `jawa/get_defs defs="Type/Name"` (STRING; read success/foundCount/notFound), `jawa/drain_log`, `jawa/letter_list`, `jawa/inspect_string`, `jawa/comp_read`, `jawa/static_call`, `jawa/mod_settings_field`, `jawa/depth_grid_set`, `jawa/fire_incident`, `jawa/spawn_pawn`, `jawa/list_things`. ## STILLSAND_SKELETONS_REMAINDER_1  (owes A3 L2, A4 L2, A5 L3)
- **A3 L2 burial**: on a Stillsand map (`jawa/world_tile_set biome=RM_Stillsand` + `jawa/world_commit` + debug action "Regenerate Current Map", the proven recipe in `Stillsand/validation.py` header) spawn a skeleton (`jawa/spawn_pawn`/`jawa/spawn_batch` of `RM_OommokSkeleton`; classes `Building_GiantSkeleton`), `jawa/depth_grid_set grid=sand depth=0.7 rect="x,z,w,h"` over its `OccupiedRect` (Odyssey-only; read `actualDepth` of each cell), then step >= 2500 ticks (`BurialCheckInterval`; `UpdateBurial()` is an instance method, so `static_call` cannot reach it - the building ticks it itself). Expect `jawa/inspect_string` on the skeleton to contain the burial line (see `GetInspectString`, `RM_GiantSkeletons.cs:204`) and Player.log/`jawa/drain_log` message `"<label>: the dunes have buried it to its top arcs."`; then `depth_grid_set depth=0` and expect `"...stripped it clean again."`. Harp silence: no direct reader (`RM_GiantSkeletons.cs:275` gates the sustainer on `Buried`) -> read the inspect line only; mark harp as UNMEASURED.

Set `horizonWarningHours` via `jawa/mod_settings_field` (it uses 0.5 in the chain). Expect a letter whose label starts `"Dust on the horizon:"` (`jawa/letter_list`), no herd pawns until the delay passes, and `RimMandrake.Stillsand.RM_HorizonWarning.PassersHonoured` rising. NOTE: `jawa/static_call` reaches only public static METHODS (and is GM-gated), `mod_settings_field` only reads the Mod class, so the static FIELD `PassersHonoured` has NO reader today -> add a one-line public static getter (or [Tool]) before this can be decided, else fall back to the herd pawns' first position (`jawa/list_pawns`) vs the letter's bearing. Control arm: `horizonPassersEnabled=false` leaves PassersHonoured flat.

## DEEPS_FAUNA_MECHANICS_1 (A1 grapple, A2 drinker sacs, A3 soulchime stun/soothe/shard armor)
Mod: `mandrake.rm.creaturebehaviors` (composed into biomes). Creatures are SWBestiary defs (`mandrake.rsw.swbestiary`, loadAfter creaturebehaviors): `RSW_BovineBeetle` (RM_CompProperties_Grappler), `RSW_BloodropMoth`/Gembug/GlowSlug (RM_CompProperties_FluidSacs + RM_HydrocarbonBloodExtension), `RSW_FacetMothLarvae` (ProximityPsychicStun + PlantAlarm + ShardArmor + TameSootheAura). NO validation chain for these three comps exists (CreatureBehaviors/validation.py chains: track_grid, mechanic_toggles, settings, every_shipped_def_reads_back) -> every row below needs a NEW chain/prove script; none is offline-decidable.
Tier: acc_green_min2 (biomes + swbestiary + rut.patches + mlie.starwarsanimalcollection + horrors) or acc_green_min (also has swbestiary). acc_biomes alone lacks swbestiary -> RSW_* creatures would not resolve. Quicktest map (L2) on a loaded list; no world needed.
- **A1** grapple lands: Check: `jawa/get_defs defs="ThingDef/RSW_BovineBeetle"` (read success/foundCount/notFound; confirm comp present), then on a quicktest map `jawa/spawn_pawn` beetle manhunter (set `mentalState`/faction hostile) beside a colonist, drive ticks via `rimworld/step_game_ticks`, read `jawa/list_pawns` -> pawns[].health.hediffs[].def (NOT `jawa/pawn_health`, which is a WRITE tool: add/remove hediff) for hediff `RM_Grappled` + a Torso injury; then a third pawn hit (jawa/pawn_use_verb or ordered_job AttackMelee) -> `RM_Grappled` gone. Expected strings: hediff defName `RM_Grappled` (RM_Hediff_Grappled, climbs to lethalSeverity) and Torso `Blunt`/crush injury. TRAP (root-caused 2026-09-19): beetle has wildness 0.05, no predator flag -> its AI re-asserts Flee over a forced AttackMelee every tick; it dies before landing a hit. Use a MANHUNTER mental state (`jawa/pawn_force_mental_break` with the Manhunter state; read back via `jawa/pawn_mental`) or an ordered job AFTER setting the pawn drafted-hostile; otherwise a false UNMEASURED/FAIL. Design call open (predator:true vs manhunter-only) is a BENCH/owner call, not a test fix.
- **A2** drinker sacs fire: spawn `RSW_BloodropMoth` (or the drinker the comp is on) + a Gembug target; a landed bite must be tagged `RSW_BloodSuck`/Bite (prior run only got generic Bruise: multi-tool RNG) -> read `jawa/list_pawns` health.hediffs on the moth for `RM_FluidSacks` (severity>0) and on victim `RM_Hediff_Drained`; kill the moth, `jawa/list_things` for `RM_DrainedFluids` (drop scaled by severity). Use many moths (`jawa/spawn_batch`) -- one pawn's tool roll is RNG. Expected: `RM_FluidSacks` present with severity read.
- **A3** soulchime: place `RSW_FacetMothLarvae` with a pawn in LoS (GenSight.LineOfSight), tick, expect hediff `PsychicShock` (stun) on pawn; control a pawn with PsychicSensitivity 0 (deaf; set via `jawa/pawn_stats`) gets none. Soothe: TameSootheAura mental-state calm on nearby animal; shard armor: `jawa/pawn_stats`/`thing_stats` ArmorRating_Sharp > base. 2026-09-19 run: victim stood adjacent 2000 ticks, no PsychicShock -> suspect the damage-trigger / LoS gating, so READ `jawa/drain_log` / Player.log first exception (not the loudest) before concluding.
- Offline-decided? No. Closest static: `modcheck` chain `mechanic_toggles` PASS for the toggles, `settings` toggle_roundtrip_soulchime* PASS (soulchimePsychicStunEnabled/TameSoothe/ShardArmor, grapplerHoldEnabled, drinkerFluidSacsEnabled) in CreatureBehaviors_20261007T203753Z.json -> proves wiring/toggles only, not behaviour. Do NOT propose rimflow verify.

## LEANINGSCRUB_VISSLER_ARM_SCAVENGERS_1
owes: A2 L2, A3 GREEN-MIN (A1 L1 verified acc_l1x-34). Mod LeaningScrub (folded in mandrake.rm.biomes, wave 0 entry) -> tier `acc_biomes` (or acc_green_min where LeaningScrub ran: Transient/modcheck/LeaningScrub_20261007T193012Z.json, `fauna/*` `bloom/*` PASS, `dripping/dripping_survives_harvest` FAIL, forms/* UNMEASURED "No public static ProofForm"). Mechanism (shipped): arm `RM_VisslerArm` is a vanilla meat ingestible that rots; `RM_VisslerArmPatches.IngestibleNow_Postfix` only gates it on setting `visslerArmFoodEnabled`; hungry animals use the ENGINE's own AI. The item's "ruled scavengers" are NOT recorded in item prose (open questions 1-3 remain unresolved there) -> the check can only assert "any hungry meat-eater", plus whichever species the owner names. Current component `vissler_arm_is_rotting_meat` only checks CompProperties_Rottable; it says "Whether a hungry animal walks to it is NOT asserted (unmeasured live by design)".
- **A2 L2**: deciding = `jawa/spawn_batch ops="RM_VisslerArm:x,z,1"` on a cleared pad; `jawa/spawn_pawn kindDef=RM_Shokka|RM_Surrik|RM_Fuzzviper|RM_Ribbonwhip faction=none` near; force hunger (`jawa/pawn_need` set food 0.1 / pawn_health), `rimworld/step_game_ticks 600`; read `jawa/pawn_census ids=<predator ids>` -> `job.def` in {Ingest, FoodDeliver...} with `job.targetA.def == "RM_VisslerArm"` (or `job.targetA.thingId` == arm id). Control: same with `visslerArmFoodEnabled=false` (`jawa/mod_settings_field typeName=RimMandrake.LeaningScrub.RM_LeaningScrubSettings`) -> no Ingest of the arm; and a non-hungry control animal ignores. State read only (owner rule: no screenshot hunt). Expected `job.def=="Ingest"` targetA arm for >=1 species; Rollbug: confirm defName via `jawa/get_defs defs="PawnKindDef/RM_Rollbug"` first (not verified).
- **A3 GREEN-MIN**: `design/validation_walks/RimMandrake/LeaningScrub.md` is DRAFT and has NO bar for arm->scavengers (only "visslers drop RM_VisslerArm" via bloom.bloom_answers_a_walker). Needs a new walk line + `validation.py` component (agents may author; the owner's validated-hash bars stay his), then run on the min list. Not offline-decided.
- Traps: wild animals may flee/attack pad; arm rots (rot timer) - read within a few thousand ticks; kill hostiles; python.exe CRLF.


## MIASMA_MECHANICS_1
owes: A1 L2 (M2 surge/salt-line moves), A2 L2 (M3 stranding pools appear + persist after recede), A3 L2 (M6 RM_WardenMother never leaves anchor). Mods: Miasma (`mandrake.rm.miasma`, biome `RM_Miasma`, incident `RUT_Surge` -> GameCondition `RUT_GradientSurge`, weather `RUT_SurgeWeather`) + engines in `EnvironmentalHazards` (`RM_MapComponent_GradientAxis`, `RM_MapComponent_StrandingPools`, `RM_CompTerritorialAnchor` duty `RM_AnchorGuard` radius 16). Both folded in `mandrake.rm.biomes` -> tier `acc_biomes` (BRIDGE + biomes + gimmesomeslack). Quicktest map on a Miasma biome tile (pattern: Stillsand `site` chain: `jawa/world_tile_set tiles=<tile> biome=RM_Miasma` + `jawa/world_commit` + regenerate; GenSteps (gradient axis, creche scatterer w/ AnchoredPawn warden) only run at map GEN so the regen is essential). No acceptance_map row; no Transient/modcheck/Miasma_* ever. `Miasma/validation.py` covers only settings round-trips, decay cell/glaze/balm/mothers-price via `jawa/static_call type="RimMandrake.Miasma.RM_MiasmaProof"`; EnvironmentalHazards/validation.py `map_mechanics` lists `stranding_pools_and_gradient_axis` as "missing instrument: a static_call proof hook on RM_MapComponent_StrandingPools ... and a Miasma map" (UNMEASURED stub).
- **A1 surge/salt line moves**: fire `jawa/fire_incident` defName `RUT_Surge` (or `jawa/game_condition`), then read axis over ticks. NO reader exists: must add static proof hook (e.g. `RM_GradientAxisProof.Read(Map)` returning `shiftInProgress`, salinity sample at fixed cells, `SaltLineCells().Count`/mean x) -> via `jawa/static_call`. Alternatives today: `jawa/get_terrain_batch rects=...` before/after (surge repaints `RM_MiasmaSaltCrust`, RM_GradientAxisRepaint) -> distinctTerrains/cell counts change; `jawa/map_info`/weather read `RUT_SurgeWeather` forced. Expected: salt-line cell centroid shifts by `TotalDelta` over the condition Duration (ramp), then recedes by `recedeDelta` (<100%: residual drift - `residualFraction`); `LastRecedeCompletedTick` advances. Terrain-batch route is usable now (no new C#) but weaker.
- **A2 stranding pools appear, persist after recede**: armed by M2's recede (`LastRecedeCompletedTick`), `RM_MapComponent_StrandingPools.MapComponentTick` on TickRare. Needs `RM_StrandingPool` list reader (pools[].cells/occupants/decayTotalTicks) -> new static_call hook, or `jawa/get_terrain_batch` for pool terrain after recede plus `jawa/list_pawns` for stranded young (`RM_*Juv`). Expected: >=1 pool after recede; still present N ticks later (<decayTotalTicks); occupants stranded. Strand needs spawned fauna: `jawa/spawn_pawn kindDef=RM_CrimsonOpeeJuv|RM_ThornbackColoJuv|...` (4 young defs in chain `nursery_young_free_tier`).
- **A3 warden never leaves anchor**: after regen, `jawa/list_pawns` / `jawa/pawn_census` -> find kind `RM_WardenMother`; record (x,z); `rimworld/step_game_ticks` in chunks (e.g. 10 x 2500); each sample distance to anchor <= 16 (anchorRadius) and pawn still alive/`spawned`. Warden is a GenStep-placed set-piece (`RM_SetPieceElement_AnchoredPawn.SetAnchor()`), so a spawn_pawn'd one has NO anchor - must use a regenerated map. Needs the anchor coordinate: only via static hook or the first sample (assume first position = anchor). PASS only if the check can fail (control: tame/move her with `jawa/` teleport and see she returns within N ticks). `jawa/pawn_census` -> `job.def`/`duty.def == RM_AnchorGuard`.
- Offline-decided? NO (all three are live map behaviour; kit logic in `MiasmaKernel`/`RM_AxisKernel` has pure selftests but those are not the criteria). 
- Trap: UtinniPatches (`RUT_Miasma.xml` BiomeDef patch) is a campaign layer; the free tier (`mandrake.rm.biomes` alone) must carry RM_Miasma + surge; salt crust/young free-tier chains are separate UNMEASURED stubs. Python.exe CRLF. Level: L2 quicktest map, not full list.

Check = screenshot of the spawned generator and the three module items (`jawa/spawn_batch`, `screenshot_cell_rect`) judged "placeholder acceptable?" (owner said art owed separately); `chain shipped_defs` does not test texture resolution. Level L3.

Expected: control gains Heatstroke, drifter does not; toggled off -> both. Instrument for AmbientTemperature offset itself missing (EXPLICIT in validation docstring).

Offline: `python3 src/RimMandrake/TheForge/selftest_theforge.py` (A1 basis) -- not run this pass. Traps: python.exe CRLF; settings must be restored (`_settings` context); RM_GameCondition_ForgeCycle phases take thousands of ticks (`jawa/time_set_ticks`).

## Section 6
# Section 6 (chunk6: 18 items, 41 owed criteria)

Conventions: composed biomes mod = `mandrake.rm.biomes` (folds Abyss, Stillsand, RustCathedral, FeverWood, TheForge, Greentide, MovingDunes, TheRot, Scarlands(key Warscar), etc.; manifest `src/RimMandrake/Biomes.compose.json`). Tiers verified with `modset_builder.py --tier X` plan output (no --apply): `mandrake.rm.biomes` is in acc_biomes, acc_green_min, acc_green_min2, acc_harness, acc_l1x, baroque_wave0, builds_biomes, beastmechanics, shrublandfauna, desertplants, pyrelands, leaningscrub. `mandrake.rm.flowworks` and `mandrake.rm.gimmesomeslack` ride in every acc_* tier. `mandrake.rut.patches` (Utinni layer) is in acc_green_min/min2/harness/l1x, NOT in acc_biomes/baroque_wave0. Generic bridge reads: `jawa/get_defs defs="Type/Name"` (STRING; read success/foundCount/notFound), `jawa/drain_log`, `jawa/letter_list`, `jawa/inspect_string`, `jawa/comp_read` (ThingComp only), `jawa/static_call` (public static METHODS only, GM build only, args `|`-separated; fields and properties are NOT reachable), `jawa/mod_settings_field`, `jawa/depth_grid_set`, `jawa/fire_incident`, `jawa/spawn_pawn`, `jawa/list_things`. Bridge only via python.exe from WSL; pipe through `tr -d '\r\n'`. L2 = quicktest map (no cold world), L1 = load only. acceptance_map rows exist only for Greentide among these items (A4 unmapped).

## STILLSAND_SKELETONS_REMAINDER_1  (owes A3 L2, A4 L2, A5 L3)
- **A3 L2 burial**: on a Stillsand map (`jawa/world_tile_set biome=RM_Stillsand` + `jawa/world_commit` + debug action "Regenerate Current Map"; recipe in `Stillsand/validation.py` header) spawn a skeleton (`RM_OommokSkeleton`, class `Building_GiantSkeleton`), `jawa/depth_grid_set grid=sand depth=0.7 rect="x,z,w,h"` over its footprint (Odyssey-only; read `actualDepth` per cell), then step >= 2500 ticks (`BurialCheckInterval`; `UpdateBurial()` is an INSTANCE method, so `static_call` cannot reach it - the building ticks it itself). Expect `jawa/inspect_string` to carry the burial line (`RM_GiantSkeletons.cs:204`) and message `"<label>: the dunes have buried it to its top arcs."`; then `depth_grid_set depth=0` and `"...stripped it clean again."`. Harp silence has no reader (sustainer gated on `Buried`, line 275) -> UNMEASURED for that clause.
  Tier acc_biomes (or baroque_wave0 = Stillsand northstar tier). L2. Offline: `python3 src/RimMandrake/Utils/selftest_skeleton_burial.py` (28 checks) decides only the hysteresis kernel -> not OFFLINE-DECIDED. `Stillsand/validation.py` `remainder_chain` has no burial arm: add one.
- **A4 L2 herd horizon**: `jawa/fire_incident` HerdMigration on the Stillsand map; copy the working raid arm in `Stillsand/validation.py:2013 horizon_chain`; set `horizonWarningHours` with `jawa/mod_settings_field` (the chain uses 0.5). Expect a letter whose label starts `"Dust on the horizon:"` (`jawa/letter_list`), herd absent until the delay passes, then entering at the announced bearing. `RM_HorizonWarning.PassersHonoured` is a public static FIELD: no reader exists (static_call is methods-only) -> add a one-line static getter or compare the herd's first position (`jawa/list_pawns`) with the letter's bearing. Control: `horizonPassersEnabled=false` leaves it flat. Tier acc_biomes. Trap: `TryFindRandomPawnEntryCell` fallback is silent ("bearing may be off").
- **A5 L3**: eyes (sand-tinted look vs real drift); `jawa/take_screenshot` for the owner, owner present. Not decidable offline.
Tier: acc_biomes; A3/A4 L2 quicktest map, A5 L3 joint.

## ABYSS_LIGHTFALL_BROOD_WRECK_1  (owes A9 L2, A10 L2, A11 L2, A12 L2, A13 L3)
Mod `mandrake.rm.abyss` folded into `mandrake.rm.biomes`. - **A10 L2**: bill at `RM_RescueShipWreck` (recipe worker `RM_RecipeWorker_WreckSalvage`): `jawa/bill_add` + `jawa/do_bill_now`; expect stock (`RM_WreckStockExtension.stock`) down (wreck `jawa/inspect_string` lists left) and message `"The brood-mother shifts in her sleep (...)"` at 45%. Refused part (bridge console, grav-field lattice, rescue berth): `RM_CompUseEffect_FitToShip.CanBeUsedBy` reason text (`RM_ShipWreck.cs:138`) via the disabled gizmo; read the exact string from source.
- **A11 L2**: spawn `RM_SummEgg` with and without a player-owned `RM_SummGreatBone` on substructure, advance the 20-day hatch (`rimworld/step_game_ticks`); expect letter `"The summing has imprinted"` vs `"The summing hatched wild"` (`jawa/letter_list`; `RM_BroodEgg.cs:165`). Stolen egg at home: message `"A Witchfire storm is gathering over the colony..."` then letter `"A summ has come for the egg"` (5-9 day timer: force ticks).
- **A12 L2**: push the meter past the hidden line (salvage everything + take the egg; L0 proves that always wakes her). Expect letter `"Summ the All-Render is awake"`, pawn `RM_SummAllRender` (`jawa/list_pawns`), `jawa/pawn_mental` = ManhunterPermanent, and ~3% damage taken (`jawa/pawn_health`). Do not fight her unattended.
- **A13 L3**: owner joint session (scale, feel, greed line). Not offline-decidable.
Tier: acc_biomes, L2 (generated lair map) + L3. L1 (A8) already passed on acc_l1x-34.

## RUSTCATHEDRAL_HULL_BOLTS_BUILD_1  (owes A2-A9, all L2)
Mod `mandrake.rm.rustcathedral` in `mandrake.rm.biomes`. Tier **acc_biomes** (also baroque_wave0/acc_l1x; none dedicated). No acceptance_map row (`acceptance_map/RustCathedral.json` has only BASE_FINISH rows). All need a gravship on a quicktest map (`jawa/gravship_launch_check`, `jawa/gravship_launch`, `jawa/gravship_land`, `jawa/gravship_status`) -> L2. Settings via `jawa/mod_settings_field` on the RustCathedral settings class: `hullBoltsEnabled hullBoltBoardMin hullBoltBoardMax hullBoltNoneChance hullBoltEdgePull hullBoltWitnessEnabled hullBoltWeightScale hullBoltIrritationCap hullBoltRealiseDays hullBoltRevealDays hullBoltPetMemoryEnabled` (`RustCathedralHumSettings.cs:81-91`). First: `jawa/get_defs defs="ThingDef/RM_HullBolt"` (success, foundCount=1; also `PawnKindDef/RM_HullBolt`, `ThoughtDef/RM_HullBoltsSeen`, `LetterDef/RM_HullBoltsDilemma`, `JobDef/RM_HullBoltTell`, `JobDef/RM_PryHullBolt`).
GAP common to A4/A5/A6/A8: the ledger `RM_GameComponent_HullBoltWitness` (public fields `total`, `entries`, `log`, `revealSent`) and the mark set `RM_MapComponent_HullBoltMarks` have NO reader (GameComponent/MapComponent; static_call is methods-only). Add `public static string ProofHullBolts()` to `Hum/RM_RustCathedralHumProof.cs` (existing pattern: `jawa/static_call type=RimMandrake.RustCathedral.Hum.RM_RustCathedralHumProof method=ProofLadder|ProofDrain|ProofWatched`). `RM_HullBolts.HullBoltsOn(Map)` and `AnyHullBoltExists()` ARE public static -> `static_call method=HullBoltsOn args=current`.
- **A2 board**: set `hullBoltBoardMin=hullBoltBoardMax=2`, `hullBoltNoneChance=0` (= "count forced 2"; `Board(engine, forcedCount)` takes an engine so it is not static_call-able). Put living bolts (`RM_WatchedBolts.LivingBoltDefName`) within 4 cells of the engine's substructure on a generated RM_RustCathedral map (`world_tile_set biome=RM_RustCathedral`, commit, Regenerate), launch and land elsewhere. Expect `HullBoltsOn args=current` -> exactly 2 `RM_HullBolt`, each on substructure (`jawa/get_gravship_substructure` / `jawa/get_terrain_batch`); step 30000 ticks, re-read: still 2, none downed (`jawa/pawn_health`). Trap: only the `GenerateGravship` prefix on a real launch swaps them in.
- **A3 resist**: hold -80C then +90C 10000 ticks each (`jawa/game_condition` / map temperature debug, `rimworld/step_game_ticks`); expect `jawa/pawn_health` on each bolt with no Hypothermia/Heatstroke/ToxicBuildup. Damage factors Flame/EMP <= 0.1: ALREADY decided offline (L0) by `python3 src/RimMandrake/RustCathedral/validation.py` -> `hullbolts_problems` ("RM_HullBolt damage factor ... <= 0.1"; def sets 0.05). Only the hediff half is live-only.
- **A4 witness**: `jawa/trade_execute` sells one `RM_BoltShedCuriosity` with a hull bolt on the map; expect ledger `entries` +1, `total` + weight x `hullBoltWeightScale`, and each bolt on `JobDef RM_HullBoltTell` (`jawa/pawn_get`). Control: same sale with none aboard adds nothing. Needs the GAP reader.
- **A5 consequence**: seed ledger T, land on a Rust Cathedral map; within the first 250-tick check expect irritation raised by min(T, `hullBoltIrritationCap`) (`ProofLadder` shows irritation/standing; or ProofHullBolts), ledger empty, letter "Uneasy hum" (`jawa/letter_list`). Needs the GAP reader.
- **A6 realise**: set `hullBoltRealiseDays` ~0.1, step; `jawa/inspect_string` on the bolt must contain `"The hull is cold under it."` (`RM_HullBolts.cs:519`); mark set coverage needs the GAP reader.
- **A7 free-tier dilemma**: `jawa/letter_list` for LetterDef `RM_HullBoltsDilemma`: exactly one, only after a hum reader is on a hull bolt's map and a tell occurs (`hullBoltRevealDays`); control: no reader -> none. Use acc_biomes (no mandrake.rut.patches = the free tier).
- **A8 pry**: order `JobDef RM_PryHullBolt` (`jawa/order_pawn`); expect 1 new `RM_BoltShedCuriosity` (`jawa/list_things`), bolt pawn gone, ledger + pry weight; with none left a later sale adds no entry. The float-menu option only appears after the reveal. GAP reader.
- **A9 toggles**: flip each `hullBolt*Enabled`/`hullBoltsEnabled` and repeat its arm; exactly that effect disappears (off -> `Board` swaps nothing; witness off -> no ledger; pet memory off -> no `RM_HullBoltsSeen`). Long.
Tier acc_biomes; L2 (quicktest map + launched gravship) and a new Proof helper first. Not offline-decidable beyond the L0 statics.

## FEVERWOOD_BROOD_RANSOM_1  (owes A3, A4, A6, A7, all L2)
Mod `mandrake.rm.feverwood` folded into `mandrake.rm.biomes`. A6/A7 are the CAMPAIGN half (`UtinniPatches/Patches/RUT_BroodRansom_Campaign.xml`, `RUT_SporefallDisplayTank`, faction `RUT_Jawa_WildsteamClan`) so they need `mandrake.rut.patches`: tier **acc_green_min** (acc_harness/acc_l1x also). A3/A4 need only biomes: **acc_biomes**. No new tier. Existing script: `FeverWood/validation.py` chain `brood_ransom` (cask inspect line + toggle only). L1 first: `jawa/get_defs defs="ThingDef/RM_SekkulaathYoungCask"` foundCount=1.
- **A3 tally**: `jawa/static_call type=RimMandrake.FeverWood.RM_WorldComponent_DeepYoung method=RestlessnessLine` -> `"Young of the deep held in the world: N (...)"` (null when `broodRansomEnabled` is false). Expect N=3 with 2 occupied `RM_SekkulaathTank` (occupied flag: `jawa/comp_read thing=RM_SekkulaathTank comp=CapturedSpecimen members=occupied`) + 1 `RM_SekkulaathYoungCask`; the tally recomputes only every 15000 ticks or on release, so step or release first. Then "Return to the deep" with the young entering a pool: N=2. Sporefall +1 only on the campaign list and while unfreed. The count itself has no [Tool] (validation.py docstring says so); the string is the read that exists.
- **A4 gift**: release a young (`released` flag) next to a `RUT_FeverWoodMirrorPool` cell (`jawa/set_terrain`), step <= 2500 ticks (gift scheduled 600-2200 after entry). Expect exactly one new thing from `RM_DeepGiftLoot` (ShipChunk, AncientSealedCrate, Gravcore, ArchiteCapsule, AIPersonaCore) within 6 cells (`jawa/list_things` BEFORE/AFTER, these may pre-exist) and letter `RM_DeepGiftLetter`. Controls: plain water and an escaped (not released) young -> 0.
- **A6 Sporefall**: `RM_GenStep_DisplayTank` places `RUT_SporefallDisplayTank` on a `Base_Faction` map of `RUT_Jawa_WildsteamClan` named Sporefall: needs that settlement generated (`jawa/inhabited_settlement_create` / `jawa/world_settlements_import`). Expect the tank (`jawa/get_defs`, `jawa/list_things`) owned by the faction; "Free the young" -> `jawa/faction_goodwill_check` drop >= `broodDisplayTankGoodwillLoss` (default 50) plus the town letter. Tier acc_green_min.
- **A7 trade**: `jawa/trade_price_probe` / generate settlement trader stock repeatedly and `jawa/list_things` for `RM_SekkulaathYoungCask` priced Expensive (stockChance 0.6, statistical); control: non-Wildsteam outlander traders never stock it (`onlyFactions`). Tier acc_green_min.
Offline: `python3 src/RimMandrake/FeverWood/selftest_feverwood_broodcampaign.py` (18 reads + 8 planted breaks) decides kernels only -> not OFFLINE-DECIDED. Tier acc_biomes (A3/A4) / acc_green_min (A6/A7); all L2.

## Section 7
# Section 7 (chunk7, 18 items)

## Shared facts (apply to all FIRST_SCRIPT items)
- Mechanism: `cd src/RimMandrake/Utils; MODCHECK_SKIP_DEPLOY=1 RIMFLOW_SEAT=FOUNDRY python3 -m modcheck.cli run <Mod>` writes `Transient/modcheck/<Mod>_<UTC>.json` (+ .html); verdict = `d["summary"]["chains"][*]["components"][*]["verdict"]` (NOT `d["chains"]`: top keys are mod/stamp/summary). `modcheck run` swaps ModsConfig (Charter expensive-list action); `modset_builder.py --apply` refuses within 3 min of Player.log touch (kill game first).
- Live-run evidence ALREADY EXISTS for 14 of 17 mods from the 2026-10-07 acc_green_min sitting: `Transient/green_min_runs_20261007.md` (per-mod result path, PASS/FAIL/UNM counts, FAIL classification). Verify records for it were written (config `acc_green_min-36`) but several against the WRONG criterion id: e.g. Abyss/Gizka verify says criterion=A2 while owes A1; Scarlands/ShipVermin/Titanic/Warcasket/RiverColors/ScarlandsLadder/Shokkweave say A3 while owes A2; Pyrinth says A4 while owes A3. Candidate: re-issue `rimflow verify <ID> --criterion <owed id> --config acc_green_min-36 --level GREEN-MIN --evidence <results json>` (do NOT run here); the live-run criterion's own evidence is the results JSON named below, the classified-FAIL criterion is the .md.
- Tier `acc_green_min` (36 mods: BRIDGE, gimmesomeslack, mandrake.rm.biomes [composed: Abyss, Scarlands(warscar), Stillsand, LeaningScrub, GelatinousSlime, CreatureBehaviors, Greentide...], swbestiary, rut.patches, rut.ashkarrflora, alphaanimals, mlie.sac, mlie.horrors, shipvermin, titaniccreatures, warcasket, rsw.gizkastowaway, oasismaker, rut.rivercolors, rut.scarlandsladder, rut.shokkweaveeconomy, gravshiplanding, pyrinth). `acc_green_min2` = biomes + CreatureBehaviors/Greentide/PyrelandsMechanics/LuminousPigment/Ninefold. Smaller: `acc_biomes` (3 wanted/10 deps: biomes+gss). None of these is a per-mod "smallest" tier except where noted.
- Traps: bridge only via python.exe from WSL (modcheck handles it); `| tr -d '\r\n'` on any python.exe capture; one shared map in the batch caused cross-run contamination (Scarlands/Warcasket/ShipVermin/Abyss site FAILs) -> run per mod on a fresh quicktest map or rerun individually; modcheck status shows RED for most because "last run failed" (a FAIL/UNMEASURED anywhere = not all_green), which is separate from GREEN-MIN criteria text (run recorded + FAILs classified).
- Nothing in this chunk is OFFLINE-DECIDABLE: each criterion text says "live"/"recorded live run". Offline parts (A1 L0 floor) are already met.

## Section 8
# Section 8 -- 18 items, 34 owed criteria (all GREEN-MIN except GRAFFITI_NORTHSTAR A2=L4, UTINNI_DISCOVERY A3=L3 A4=L4)

## Shared mechanics (apply to every item below unless the item says otherwise)

- Every "first script" item: A-run criterion = "one live run produced a results JSON" ; A-classify = "every non-pass classified".
  Deciding instrument is NOT a single bridge call: it is the suite's chain/component verdicts in the results JSON
  (`Transient/modcheck/<Mod>_<utc>.json`, `summary.chains[].components[].verdict`; northstar-driver runs write `Transient/northstar/<Mod>_<utc>.json`).
- Live command (Windows python only, bridge held, game up on the tier, quicktest map): `python.exe src/RimMandrake/Utils/modcheck/cli.py run <Mod>`
  (composes folded mods via Biomes.compose.json + `tier_package_ids`; REWRITES ModsConfig -> expensive-list action, game must be down first)
  or, for mods with a `northstar_plan.py`: `python.exe src/RimMandrake/Utils/northstar_driver/cli.py run --mod <Mod> --plan <plan>` (never touches ModsConfig; you must already be on the tier).
- Offline decision path AFTER a run exists: `infrastructure/state/acceptance_map/<Mod>.json` rows (none exist for any of this chunk's mods yet; existing tables: EmpirePursuit FallLineArrivals FloodedCanyon Greentide LuminousPigment PyrelandsMechanics RustCathedral Stillsand). Proposed row pattern (copy Greentide/PyrelandsMechanics): run-criterion -> `components:["@run"] mode:all`; classify-criterion -> `components:["*"] mode:classified`, plus a `--classified file.json` `{"chain/component":"harness|site|mod|unmeasured: why"}` written after reading failures. Then `python3 src/RimMandrake/Utils/acceptance_map.py check` / `apply` (apply --record is what calls verify; do NOT run).
- Classification criteria can never be OFFLINE-DECIDED without a run: with no non-pass it passes trivially, with non-pass it needs the classified file.
- Tiers: composed `mandrake.rm.biomes` folds CreatureBehaviors EnvironmentalHazards Wreckage Abyss BlueDesert Cauldron Contagion DivingInteraction FeverWood FloodedCanyon GelatinousSlime Greentide LanternDeeps LeaningScrub LongShade Miasma MovingDunes NightsideIce Pyrelands RustCathedral Scarlands SeaShores Stillsand TerminalBiomes TheForge TheRot TheSump Wasteland Webwork WeepingStones. Tier `acc_biomes` (MEASURED via modset_builder.resolve_tier: biomes + gimmesomeslack + flowworks + luminouspigment) loads all folded mods below.
- Traps: python.exe CRLF (`| tr -d '\r\n'`); bridge only via python.exe (WSL NAT); `jawa/get_defs defs` is a STRING "DefType/DefName" ("A;B" for several) -- read `success`/`foundCount`/`notFound`, a failed call reads as ABSENT to a substring check; quicktest map vs full list (these suites spawn/terrain-edit -> need a quicktest map, L2); `modset_builder --apply` refuses within 3 min of Player.log activity (kill game first); resolve_tier prints a benign "forcing mandrake.rm.biomes ... unmet ordering on mandrake.rut.patches" warning.
- Player.log decisive strings common to all: first of `Could not find type named`, `Could not resolve cross-reference`, `Config error in`, `NullReferenceException` after the `Bridge token:` line. A missing comp TYPE discards the whole def silently.

## Section 9
# Section 9 (chunk9: 18 items, 39 owed criteria)

## Shared conventions (apply to every FIRST_SCRIPT item below unless overridden)
- **G-A2 (a "live run recorded" criterion)** deciding check, after `python.exe src/RimMandrake/Utils/northstar_driver/cli.py run --mod <M> --plan <plan.py>` (live, bridge held, run from repo root): read `Transient/northstar/<M>_<utc>.json` -> `mode == "live"`, `len(components) > 0`, preflight `tool_census`, `game_loaded`, `no_modal` all `PASS`. Mock files (mode "mock") never count. Acceptance-map form: `{"item":..,"criterion":"A2","level":"GREEN-MIN","components":["@run"],"mode":"all"}`; NO table exists for any mod in this chunk (`infrastructure/state/acceptance_map/` holds only EmpirePursuit, FallLineArrivals, FloodedCanyon, Greentide, LuminousPigment, PyrelandsMechanics, RustCathedral, Stillsand) -> each needs a table row before `acceptance_map.py apply --record`.
- **G-A3 (a "every non-pass classified" criterion)**: a person/agent writes `classified.json` `{"chain/component":"harness|site|mod|unmeasured: why"}` after reading the run's FAIL/UNMEASURED components; acceptance_map `mode:"classified", components:["*"]` returns PASS only with that file (else UNMEASURED). NOT decidable offline until a live run exists; after the run it is a pure offline (python3) judgement -> candidate for `rimflow verify` once the file exists.
- **Plan files**: only FloodedCanyon, ExplosiveGrowth, Contagion, Wasteland, WeepingStones, Pyrelands, Bacta ship `northstar_plan.py` (`USE_SUITE = True`). Miasma, LongShade, RustCathedral, TheRot, TheSump, Sarlacc, LoreStages, FungalSoilTrade, WildsteamEggBounty have only `validation.py`: either write a 6-line `northstar_plan.py` (copy FloodedCanyon's: MOD / USE_SUITE=True / EXPECT_MODS / NEED_GOD=False / RECT=None) or record a `modcheck run` result via `python3 -m modcheck.cli record <Mod> --result <json> --tier T` (does NOT touch ModsConfig). NEVER `modcheck run <Mod>` (rewrites live ModsConfig to MINIMAL; Charter expensive-list action).
- **TRAP GimmeSomeSlack**: JawaBench `get_defs`/`map_info` TypeLoad-fail without `mandrake.rm.gimmesomeslack` (MEASURED 2026-10-07, modset_builder.py comment at TIERS["ishko"]). Old tiers `bacta`, `explosivegrowth_solo`, `weepingstones_solo`, `pyrelands`, `diving`, `baroque_wave0` do NOT list it (`acc_*` tiers do). Add it or the run is refused at `game_loaded`.
- **Composed biomes**: `mandrake.rm.biomes` folds (Biomes.compose.json): FloodedCanyon, LongShade, Miasma, RustCathedral, TheRot, WeepingStones, Contagion, Wasteland, TheSump, DivingInteraction, Pyrelands. NOT folded: FungalSoilTrade, Bacta, Sarlacc, WildsteamEggBounty, ExplosiveGrowth, LoreStages, FlowWorks. The standalone source folders are SOURCE only; the packageId to load is `mandrake.rm.biomes`.
- **Traps**: python.exe emits CRLF (`| tr -d '\r\n'`); bridge only via python.exe (WSL cannot reach loopback); `Player.log` first exception not loudest; get_defs `defs` is a STRING "DefType/DefName" and read `success`/`foundCount`/`notFound` not a substring; dry-run `--mock` runs write only to `--out` (offline, safe).
- Existing dated live evidence is `modcheck`-format (`Transient/modcheck/<Mod>_<utc>.json`, `summary.chains[].components[]`), whereas the criteria say `Transient/northstar/`. Either re-run through the driver or copy/record with `modcheck record`; decide before closing.
